独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 39165524630 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 1

轻微指引缺失上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F3 · 应用开发指引未说明部分插件运行时需保护可选插件 token

文档 / Skill / 示例 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

服务与后台任务指引只展示 provider 的 register/boot、容器解析和生命周期，没有说明部分插件 runtime 会缺失 authorizationToken，也没有点名 app-server 的 runtime 测试用空插件列表装配应用 provider。测试与当前 provider/route 的 has guard 共同支持该改进点。Scheduler 文档虽已示例 container.has，但仅限插件专属的可选集成，未覆盖主应用 provider 在测试 runtime 中的通用前提，因此不足以推翻本条，只把结论缩窄为“主应用 provider 指引缺少测试前提与通用守卫说明”。

  • 职责与公开约定：services-and-jobs 把 provider 职责写成 register 绑定 token、boot 处理需要其他服务的工作，并示例 app.container.resolve；它没有承诺所有插件 token 在每个 runtime 都存在，也没有可选 token 守卫或测试前提。这里应作为指引缺口而不是 API 违约：容器负责装配，应用负责判断哪些集成是可选的。 E63
  • 实际行为证据：E61 的测试保留 appRuntime.serviceProviders，同时把 plugins 置空并只创建一个 testing plugin；E72 表明该 serviceProviders 只含 DocumentsProvider，所以 boot 会在没有 authorizationToken 的容器中执行。E45 显示当前 boot 用 container.has 跳过授权注册，E62 记录修复前无条件 resolve 会报 not registered。该改进项不要求用运行时失败来证明，但实际代码与修复路径和结论一致。 E61 E72 E45 E62
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用在 DocumentsProvider.boot 和文档路由工厂中用 container.has 显式跳过，前者在 E45，后者在 E71；这是修复轮加入的绕过，不是应用误用公开 API 或遗漏业务逻辑。问题在于模板测试的空插件前提没有被主开发指引写明，而应用代码已按该前提装配。 E45 E71 E62
  • 环境因素：该约束来自进程内测试的插件装配，不涉及外部服务、凭据、依赖安装或网络；E61 显示测试自行创建 embedded scope 并注入 serviceProviders。未运行应用，但 guidance-gap 不要求运行时复现；没有缺失的环境材料能改变该判断。 E61 E72
  • 工厂因素：该前提由工厂维护的模板测试与 Skill 文本共同构成：E61 的测试使用 app-template-default 的 embedded scope 和 testing plugin，E62 将其归为模板运行时测试，E63 则是主开发 Skill 的服务参考。没有证据表明注入配置、构建或 QA 脚本造成此现象；可归因的是模板测试约束未写入工厂 Skill，属文档改进。 E61 E62 E63
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：现有公开文档已有一处 container.has 用法：Scheduler 的 provider 示例在 boot 跳过缺失服务（E69），授权参考也提醒不要假定可选插件都存在（E70）。但前者只针对 Scheduler 的可选集成，后者没有给出 provider/route 守卫写法或 app-server 空插件测试前提，主 services-and-jobs 参考（E63）仍未覆盖。因此可以确认主应用 provider 指引有改进空间，但不能说整个仓库完全未发现该模式。 E69 E70 E63

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

services-and-jobs 指引展示了 provider 在 register 绑定 token、boot 解析其他服务，以及注册/启动阶段，但没有说明模板 runtime/provider 测试会只装配一个 testing plugin；此时应用 provider 若假定 authorizationToken 一定存在，会在 boot 抛 Service "@nocobase/app/authorization" is not registered。冻结应用在修复轮才通过 app.container.has 判断和空路由规避。

实际影响
按现有指引实现的 provider 会在模板 runtime 测试中整体启动失败，排查者需要从错误反推可选依赖处理；应用侧可绕过，但缺少统一的公开约定。
建议改法
在 services-and-jobs 或 server-routes 参考中补充：应用 provider/路由解析插件所有的可选 token 前先 app.container.has(token)，并点名 tests/logic/app-server.test.ts 的部分插件 runtime 测试；同时说明必需依赖应显式失败而非静默跳过。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
模板 runtime 测试注入 testing plugin 并保留 appRuntime.serviceProviders，使 DocumentsProvider 在缺少 Authorization 插件的环境中 boot。
应有能力 / 行为
指引应给出可选插件 token 的保护方式和测试前提，让应用在部分插件运行时能启动，同时保留完整插件集下的授权注册。
实际观察
指引只描述 register/boot 生命周期和一般性 resolve；应用最初无条件 resolve authorizationToken，测试报 Service "@nocobase/app/authorization" is not registered，修复轮才加 has guard。
应用绕行与代价
应用在 boot 用 container.has(authorizationToken) 跳过授权注册，并在路由工厂缺少 authentication/authorization token 时返回空 Hono 路由；代价是代码需自行判断哪些能力可选。
改进后的验收标准
未来在只含 non-auth testing plugin 的 runtime 中，应用启动不再抛未注册服务，application-owned /test-runtime 路由仍返回 200；完整插件集下 /api/documents 仍挂载并返回 401。
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 4 条
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md:24–60E63

指引展示 provider 注册 token、boot 解析其他服务和生命周期表，但没有说明部分插件运行时中如何保护可选插件 token。

}

export const orderServiceToken=[REDACTED] =
  createServiceToken<OrderService>('app/order-service');

export default class OrderProvider extends ServiceProvider<Application> {
  public readonly name: string = 'app/order-provider';

  public override register(): void {
    this.app.container.singleton(orderServiceToken, () => {
      const database = this.app.container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
      return createOrderService(database);
    });
  }
}
```

Add the provider to the array in `server/providers/index.ts` and export its token from there so routes can import it.

Resolve it where you need it:

```ts
const orders = app.container.resolve(orderServiceToken);
```

**The token is the identity.** Two `createServiceToken` calls with the same name are two different keys — the container matches by object identity. Always import the token from where it is defined rather than recreating one.

`singleton` builds the service once, on first resolve. Use `instance` for an already-constructed value.

Keep the layers apart: a service should not read a Hono context, return HTTP status codes, or decide retry behavior. It takes its dependencies and returns domain results.

## Provider lifecycle

| Method       | Runs                             | For                                              |
| ------------ | -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `register()` | Assembly, before anything starts | Binding tokens. Do not connect or start anything |
| `boot()`     | After all providers registered   | Work needing other services                      |

app/server/providers/documents.ts:371–385E45

DocumentsProvider.boot 在 authorizationToken 缺失时跳过授权注册；注释明确指向模板 provider/runtime 测试的部分插件运行时。

  public override async boot(): Promise<void> {
    // `authorizationToken` belongs to the Authorization plugin. This application always registers that
    // plugin, but a runtime may assemble the application's providers without it (the template's
    // provider/runtime tests do), so document authorization is contributed only when it exists.
    if (!this.app.container.has(authorizationToken)) return;

    const authz = this.app.container.resolve(authorizationToken);

    authz.recordAccess.define(ownOrPublishedAccess);
    authz.recordAccess.define(publishedPublicAccess);
    authz.recordAccess.define(nonConfidentialAccess);

    authz.database.collections.add({
      name: 'documents',
      title: { key: 'documents.title', ns: APP_NS },
上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [轻微][指引缺失] 应用开发指引未说明部分插件运行时需保护可选插件 token

**相关对象**：`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

服务与后台任务指引只展示 provider 的 register/boot、容器解析和生命周期，没有说明部分插件 runtime 会缺失 authorizationToken，也没有点名 app-server 的 runtime 测试用空插件列表装配应用 provider。测试与当前 provider/route 的 has guard 共同支持该改进点。Scheduler 文档虽已示例 container.has，但仅限插件专属的可选集成，未覆盖主应用 provider 在测试 runtime 中的通用前提，因此不足以推翻本条，只把结论缩窄为“主应用 provider 指引缺少测试前提与通用守卫说明”。

- 职责与公开约定：services-and-jobs 把 provider 职责写成 register 绑定 token、boot 处理需要其他服务的工作，并示例 app.container.resolve；它没有承诺所有插件 token 在每个 runtime 都存在，也没有可选 token 守卫或测试前提。这里应作为指引缺口而不是 API 违约：容器负责装配，应用负责判断哪些集成是可选的。；证据：E63（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md:24–60）
- 实际行为证据：E61 的测试保留 appRuntime.serviceProviders，同时把 plugins 置空并只创建一个 testing plugin；E72 表明该 serviceProviders 只含 DocumentsProvider，所以 boot 会在没有 authorizationToken 的容器中执行。E45 显示当前 boot 用 container.has 跳过授权注册，E62 记录修复前无条件 resolve 会报 not registered。该改进项不要求用运行时失败来证明，但实际代码与修复路径和结论一致。；证据：E61（app/tests/logic/app-server.test.ts:305–355）、E72（app/server/providers/index.ts:5–9）、E45（app/server/providers/documents.ts:371–385）、E62（artifacts/retro.json:1–1）
- 应用接入与业务边界：应用在 DocumentsProvider.boot 和文档路由工厂中用 container.has 显式跳过，前者在 E45，后者在 E71；这是修复轮加入的绕过，不是应用误用公开 API 或遗漏业务逻辑。问题在于模板测试的空插件前提没有被主开发指引写明，而应用代码已按该前提装配。；证据：E45（app/server/providers/documents.ts:371–385）、E71（app/server/routes/documents.ts:68–76）、E62（artifacts/retro.json:1–1）
- 环境因素：该约束来自进程内测试的插件装配，不涉及外部服务、凭据、依赖安装或网络；E61 显示测试自行创建 embedded scope 并注入 serviceProviders。未运行应用，但 guidance-gap 不要求运行时复现；没有缺失的环境材料能改变该判断。；证据：E61（app/tests/logic/app-server.test.ts:305–355）、E72（app/server/providers/index.ts:5–9）
- 工厂因素：该前提由工厂维护的模板测试与 Skill 文本共同构成：E61 的测试使用 app-template-default 的 embedded scope 和 testing plugin，E62 将其归为模板运行时测试，E63 则是主开发 Skill 的服务参考。没有证据表明注入配置、构建或 QA 脚本造成此现象；可归因的是模板测试约束未写入工厂 Skill，属文档改进。；证据：E61（app/tests/logic/app-server.test.ts:305–355）、E62（artifacts/retro.json:1–1）、E63（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md:24–60）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：现有公开文档已有一处 container.has 用法：Scheduler 的 provider 示例在 boot 跳过缺失服务（E69），授权参考也提醒不要假定可选插件都存在（E70）。但前者只针对 Scheduler 的可选集成，后者没有给出 provider/route 守卫写法或 app-server 空插件测试前提，主 services-and-jobs 参考（E63）仍未覆盖。因此可以确认主应用 provider 指引有改进空间，但不能说整个仓库完全未发现该模式。；证据：E69（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/definitions.md:16–21）、E70（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/authorization.md:9–9）、E63（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md:24–60）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

services-and-jobs 指引展示了 provider 在 register 绑定 token、boot 解析其他服务，以及注册/启动阶段，但没有说明模板 runtime/provider 测试会只装配一个 testing plugin；此时应用 provider 若假定 authorizationToken 一定存在，会在 boot 抛 Service "@nocobase/app/authorization" is not registered。冻结应用在修复轮才通过 app.container.has 判断和空路由规避。

**影响**：按现有指引实现的 provider 会在模板 runtime 测试中整体启动失败，排查者需要从错误反推可选依赖处理；应用侧可绕过，但缺少统一的公开约定。

**建议改动**：在 services-and-jobs 或 server-routes 参考中补充：应用 provider/路由解析插件所有的可选 token 前先 app.container.has(token)，并点名 tests/logic/app-server.test.ts 的部分插件 runtime 测试；同时说明必需依赖应显式失败而非静默跳过。

**验收标准**：未来在只含 non-auth testing plugin 的 runtime 中，应用启动不再抛未注册服务，application-owned /test-runtime 路由仍返回 200；完整插件集下 /api/documents 仍挂载并返回 401。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md:24–60` — 指引展示 provider 注册 token、boot 解析其他服务和生命周期表，但没有说明部分插件运行时中如何保护可选插件 token。
- `app/tests/logic/app-server.test.ts:305–355` — runtime 装配测试只注入一个 testing plugin，仍保留 appRuntime.serviceProviders；应用 provider 不能假定授权插件一定存在。
- `artifacts/retro.json:1–1` — retro 记录修复轮阻塞：DocumentsProvider.boot 无条件解析 authorizationToken 导致 Service "@nocobase/app/authorization" is not registered。
- `app/server/providers/documents.ts:371–385` — DocumentsProvider.boot 在 authorizationToken 缺失时跳过授权注册；注释明确指向模板 provider/runtime 测试的部分插件运行时。

F2 · 自建“人员与角色”页最初遗漏账号停用与会话失效入口

Agent / 业务实现 · @nocobase/app-plugin-users · @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-users/SKILL.md

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

第二轮管理员页面每行只有 Manage roles，没有 disabledAt 状态、停用/启用操作，documents.admin 也没有 user disable/enable 授权；因此 B05 无法执行或验证。Users 插件本身已有 disable + revokeSessions，问题在应用页面与授权未接上。

实际影响
离职阅读者的既有会话无法通过管理 UI 失效，安全需求 B05 在第二轮失败；这是应用接入缺口，不是框架能力缺失。
建议改法
已在修复轮补齐 disabledAt 展示、停用确认、启用操作与 documents.admin 的 user disable/enable 授权，并复用既有 POST /api/users/:userId/disable|enable。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
第二轮在 Document administration > People and roles 检查 Yi Reader 行、Manage roles 对话框、Sharing rules 和账户菜单，均无停用账号入口。
应有能力 / 行为
管理员页面应显示账号状态，并能对待离职用户执行停用；停用后所有现有会话失效且不能重新登录。
实际观察
页面只管理文档角色，用户列表不含 disabledAt，管理员 permission set 未授予 user disable/enable；B05 报告 failed。
应用绕行与代价
修复轮把页面接到 Users 的既有 disable/enable 接口，并在事务/权限层复用插件实现；未新增重复停用端点或自行删会话。
改进后的验收标准
第三轮 B05 通过：管理员停用乙后，乙原会话刷新跳转登录页、重新登录提示账号已停用，甲仍可登录并看到原有资料；documents.admin 同时拥有 disable/enable 且没有 user read。
E65 E62 E57 E53 E55 E66 E64
实现者过程与运行背景 · 2 个过程问题 / 2 条建议 · 不自动归为框架问题

本任务已处理只描述应用侧处理结果，不代表 NocoBase3 上游已修复。

本轮过程记录

修复轮：先修复文档 provider/路由在无插件运行时启动失败；随后修复“人员与角色”页缺少停用账号能力：页面只做角色管理，用户列表未带账号状态，管理员权限集也未授予 user disable/enable，导致离职读者无法停用且已有会话无法失效。现由 Users 插件在停用时统一删除会话，并补齐页面状态、停用/启用操作、授权、双语与测试。

问题 01 · 修复阶段

无插件的应用运行时启动时报 Service "@nocobase/app/authorization" is not registered

本任务已处理

tests/logic/app-server.test.ts > registers application-owned providers and routes from the runtime 在 DocumentsProvider.boot 解析 authorizationToken 时抛错；同一文件中的文档路由工厂也直接解析 authenticationToken 与 authorizationToken。

原因（实现者自述）
DocumentsProvider.boot 和 server/routes/documents.ts 无条件解析插件所有的 authenticationToken/authorizationToken。模板运行时测试用空插件列表组装真实应用运行时，boot 阶段因此抛错，路由挂载阶段也会同样失败。
处理方式
server/providers/documents.ts 的 boot() 先用 app.container.has(authorizationToken) 判断，缺失时跳过文档授权注册；server/routes/documents.ts 在 authenticationToken 或 authorizationToken 缺失时返回空 Hono 路由。完整插件集下行为不变：临时用真实 embedded 应用验证 GET /api/documents 返回 401（路由已挂载、需登录），并重跑 tests/logic/app-server.test.ts 与 tests/logic/documents-authorization.test.ts 全部通过。
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 02 · 修复阶段

“人员与角色”页没有停用账号操作，离职读者无法被停用且已有会话不会立即失效

本任务已处理

管理员在 /settings/documents 的“人员与角色”中只能管理文档角色，无法停用即将离职的读者 Yi；页面也不显示账号是否已停用。即使直接调用 Users 插件，文档管理员权限集没有 user disable/enable 授权，会被 403 拒绝。

原因（实现者自述）
应用自建的 People and roles 页面只覆盖角色分配与共享规则，遗漏了账号生命周期；后台用户列表查询、响应 schema 和客户端类型都未包含 disabledAt；文档管理员权限集只授予文档/设置权限。Users 插件已有 disable(userId) 能力（写入 disabledAt、删除全部 Session、断开 realtime、禁止后续登录），但页面和授权都没有接上，因此该安全需求无法通过 UI 完成。
处理方式
在 People and roles 增加 Account 状态列和行操作：停用前弹出确认，停用后显示 Disabled 并可直接重新启用；调用既有规范接口 POST /api/users/:userId/disable|enable，不新增重复的停用端点，也不自行删除会话。扩展 DocumentRoleAssignment/documentUserSchema/DocumentRoleUser 的 disabledAt；为 documents.admin 授予 { type: "user", id: "*" } 的 disable/enable；隐藏当前登录账号的停用入口，补充中英文文案和成功/失败提示。新增组件测试覆盖停用流程，并在权限集测试中断言管理员拥有 disable/enable 且不获得 read。
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json

实现者补充建议

来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

建议 01 · Skill 与文档

文档应说明应用 provider/路由必须容忍插件服务缺失

需人工判断

模板的 tests/logic/app-server.test.ts 会用空插件列表组装真实应用运行时，应用自有 provider 或路由工厂一旦在 boot/挂载阶段解析插件所有的服务 token 就整体失败；这一约束在现有 Skill 中未写明，只有踩到测试失败时才暴露。

建议改动

在 nocobase-app-development 的 services-and-jobs 或 server-routes 参考中加一段：应用 provider 与路由工厂在解析插件所有 token 前使用 app.container.has(token)，并点名 tests/logic/app-server.test.ts 的运行时测试会强制这一点。

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 02 · Skill 与文档

自定义用户管理页应复用 Users 插件账号生命周期并显式授予 user action

需人工判断

People and roles 这类自建页面容易只实现角色分配，却遗漏停用、启用、会话失效等账号状态；Users 插件已提供 disable/enable（disable 会删除全部 Session），但角色必须在 { type: "user", id: "*" } 上显式获得对应 action，否则页面按钮会遇到 403。现有 Skill 说明了权限授予，但没有把“停用账号会立即注销所有会话”与自建人员页的验收项联系起来。

建议改动

在 nocobase-app-plugin-users 的 Skill 中补充自建人员管理页清单：读取并展示 disabledAt、使用 POST /api/users/:userId/disable|enable、为管理角色授予 user disable/enable、确认停用会立即删除所有会话，并测试停用后原会话失效。

来源：Agent · retro.json

E46

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

资料员/阅读者只能看到并操作各自责任范围内的资料，且允许/拒绝必须由服务端真实执行。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架提供 grant、record access、字段策略和数据库 authorizer；应用声明资料范围与角色。
推荐使用方式
用 defineDatabasePermission 声明 collection 动作与字段，用 defineRecordAccess 声明 owner/published/confidential 过滤，并在服务端路由调用 authz.database.authorize。
Agent 实际使用
应用按 Skill 声明了 ownOrPublished/publishedPublic/nonConfidential、documents 复合资源和三个 permission set；B01/B02 的真实 UI 请求通过。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E40 E41 E42 E43
管理员、资料员、阅读者的页面入口与业务动作分离，阅读者不能通过编辑/创建接口绕过 UI。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架提供 action/page/composite 权限和 deny 路径；应用决定角色矩阵。
推荐使用方式
把业务动作声明为 composite action，绑定字段级数据库权限，并用 authorize（而非仅 UI can）保护路由。
Agent 实际使用
应用声明 documents.admin/maintainer/reader，记录范围与 restriction 前缀绑定在 permission set；verify-2/3 中阅读者编辑、创建、管理入口均被拒绝。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口

expand/restrict 组合顺序 · 具体记录选择 · 先有 grants

E51

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

把单份私有草稿临时共享给阅读者，撤销后该阅读者不能继续看到。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架提供 sharing rule 的 expand 语义、具体记录 selection 和管理路由；应用提供选择 UI 与清理规则。
推荐使用方式
创建 resource 为 composite、subject 为用户、selection 为 records 的 sharing rule；撤销用删除规则，下一次请求重新求值。
Agent 实际使用
应用通过 /authorization/sharingRules 创建/删除单记录规则，B03 刷新/重查后私有草稿消失；未自行扩展授权绕过。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E47 E48 E52
即使保密资料被共享，阅读者仍不得看到；restriction 必须覆盖共享的 expand 结果。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架保证 expand/restrict 组合顺序并执行 restriction resolver；应用声明保密范围。
推荐使用方式
用 restrictionRulesPlugin 的 restrict/bySubject 规则在 grants、default access、sharing 之后收窄；应用把 confidential=false 作为限制范围。
Agent 实际使用
应用为 reader 创建 non-confidential restriction 前缀规则；B04 中即使 C 被 sharing，阅读者列表仍无 C，资料员可读。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E49 E51 E52
默认访问规则应能与显式 grant、共享组合，而不是绕过或重复资源。正常组合
框架职责与业务边界
框架定义 default access 的 expand 语义与 one-per-resource 冲突；应用决定是否采用。
推荐使用方式
按需安装 default-access；同一资源只保留一条规则，资源重复时处理 DefaultAccessConflictError。
Agent 实际使用
插件已安装但文档库公开读取主要走 permission set 的 publishedPublic record access，没有强行用 default access；这是正常组合而非缺失。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口

无内置用户管理页 · disable/enable HTTP API · 自定义页面建议

E57

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

停用阅读者乙后，乙已有会话立即不能访问，重新登录被拒；资料员甲不受影响。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架提供 disable/enable、disabledAt、session 删除与登录拒绝；应用负责在自建人员页暴露状态、操作和授权。
推荐使用方式
调用 Users 的 POST /api/users/:userId/disable；disable 内部写 disabledAt 并 revokeSessions；人员页读取 disabledAt 并为管理员授予 user disable/enable。
Agent 实际使用
第二轮应用人员页只有 Manage roles，未暴露停用/状态且 documents.admin 无 user disable/enable，B05 失败；修复轮接入既有接口后第三轮通过。
缺口归因
Agent / 业务实现
E53 E54 E55 E57 E65 E66 E64
停用后重新登录必须被拒绝，而不仅是 UI 隐藏。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架在 session 创建与请求认证路径检查 disabledAt；应用不得自行删除会话或复制底层存储。
推荐使用方式
复用 Authentication 的 disable/revokeSessions 与 session hook，不新增停用端点。
Agent 实际使用
修复后的页面调用既有 disable/enable；第三轮验证原会话刷新跳登录、重新登录提示 This account is disabled。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E56 E55 E66

92/100

E53 E55 E56

一个 disable 调用同时处理状态、会话和 realtime 断开，路由语义清楚；但插件不提供管理页面，应用需自行组装 UI 和授权。

E54 E53 E57

Agent 友好度

E57 E53

设计合理性

89/100

Users 负责管理事务与角色范围，Authentication 负责身份/session 语义；disable 复用底层用户存储和 permission set 可移除检查，边界清晰。

E53 E55 E56

实现完整性与可靠性

91/100

disable 在事务中写入 disabledAt 并 revokeSessions，session create hook 对 disabledAt 与 service account 再校验；第三轮原会话和重新登录均被拒绝。

E55 E56 E53

provider register/boot 生命周期 · token 解析

E63

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

文档库的 provider、路由和授权声明必须在应用启动时可靠注册，且在测试/部分插件运行时中不整体崩溃。正常组合
框架职责与业务边界
框架负责贡献收集与 provider 阶段编排；指引应说明应用 provider 对可选插件服务的依赖前提；应用决定哪些能力是必需。
推荐使用方式
把 provider/routes 加入 runtime contributions；在 boot 中解析其他服务。当前指引未说明部分插件运行时需用 container.has 保护可选 token。
Agent 实际使用
应用 provider 最初无条件解析 authorizationToken，模板 runtime 测试因缺少授权插件抛 Service is not registered；修复轮加 container.has 后通过，并在注释中记录该前提。
缺口归因
文档 / Skill / 示例
E58 E61 E62 E63 E45
provider 注册失败或阶段顺序错误应可诊断，不能静默跳过。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架维护阶段状态、重复 name 检查与失败 shutdown；应用负责自身 boot 的异常处理。
推荐使用方式
按 register→boot→start 分阶段，register 只绑定 token，boot 做依赖服务的装配。
Agent 实际使用
应用按阶段注册 provider，修复后 boot 先检查 authorization 服务；路由在 token 缺失时返回空 Hono 路由，完整插件集行为不变。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口

Infrastructure feedback

做得好的地方与证据

值得保留的框架能力、评审覆盖范围与全部原始证据。原评审置信度与反馈复核结果分别展示，均不代替人工核验。

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 1

做得好的地方
F1 · 授权组合能直接表达文档库的角色、记录范围与保密收窄

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/authorization · @nocobase/app-plugin-authorization · @nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules

⌄

Authorization 的 record access/composite/permission set 与 sharing/restriction 插件形成可组合路径：应用声明业务范围和角色，框架在数据库 authorizer 与请求上下文中执行 allow/deny。B01-B04 的真实 UI 验收均通过，包括共享后可见、撤销后不可见、保密资料即使被共享仍被 restriction 拦截。

提供的帮助
应用无需自写一套旁路权限检查；框架承担了 grant 组合、字段校验、记录范围与 restriction 顺序。
应保留的能力
无需修改；后续指引可继续把这个“permission set + record access + sharing/restriction”组合作为企业文档场景示例。
E37 E40 E47 E49 E39 E66

问题、误导与建议统一收录在问题与改进，不在此重复展示。

本次评审的覆盖限制
  • 未查看截图，ui 状态为 not-reviewed，不能评价跨页面视觉一致性。
  • 未独立运行应用或验收脚本；B01-B05 结果来自冻结的原始 QA 报告与日志。
  • 历史日志中的选择器、脚本解析与路径探测错误只按相邻工具返回核对，未重放。
  • 未覆盖所有 relation 写路径、复杂嵌套 scope、API key/service account 与高并发规则变更。
  • 未核对最新上游是否已修改文档或测试；结论仅针对冻结快照。
  • 快照未包含 3 个超出预算、非普通文件或不可读取的文件；未据此确认其实现。

评审针对已封存候选补丁与当时的依赖；可能在原搭建内或之后单独补跑。与 PR 或 NocoBase3 当前最新提交不是同一概念。评审只读冻结材料，引用运行日志不代表评审者重新复现。此报告未执行最新上游源码复核。

Sharing does not grant the operation, fields, page access or related records, and it cannot select all records. Grants, default access and sharing combine first; restriction rules then narrow the result.\n\nRead the installed `nocobase-app-plugin-authorization` Skill first for composite resources, data scopes, fields, server policy enforcement and inherited subjects. The package README at `node_modules/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/README.md` is the complete reference for the service, the HTTP routes and the exports; this Skill covers how to use them.\n\nFor sharing between departments, read the application development Skill's `references/organization/permission-design.md` first. A rule whose recipient is a department shares specific records with it; a viewer-relative department scope such as 本部门及下属部门 is computed for the viewer and cannot select another department's records. The guide's optional sharing section covers this plugin, and its core section says what remains with permission sets alone.\n\n## Development workflow\n\n1. Identify the real collaboration exception: who receives which records, for which actions, and why. Use explicit records for one handover; use a record access selection for a maintained region or team rule.\n2. Grant the recipients the business action through a permission set first. Register inherited subjects and their active membership resolver through the main authorization Skill.\n3. Inspect every data scope the operation requires. Quote submission needs the selected quote and its actual parent project. Add each action and data scope deliberately; view, edit and submit are different permissions.\n4. Save a complete rule through the HTTP routes or the service. Keep record ids tied to their data scope's collection and preserve the other entries on update.\n5. Verify the handover works, an unshared parent still blocks the workflow, restrictions still exclude confidential records, and sharing alone cannot activate a missing operation.\n6. Revoke the recipient or the team's permission set and verify the next request loses only that source. Independent direct-user sharing and direct assignments must survive.\n\nFor example, a quote handover shares quote edit and submit plus the parent project's submit scope with a delegated engineer. Use the real record ids, never sample ones.\n\n## Install\n\nRegister the default export of `@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/client` in `client/plugins.ts` and of `@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/server` in `server/plugins.ts`, beside the main authorization plugin, and run the application's migrations. Add the factory to the `plugins` of `server/config/authorization.ts`, merged with the application's other rule factories:\n\n```ts\nimport { sharingRules } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/server';\nimport {\n  defineAppConfig,\n  type AppConfigFactory,\n} from '@nocobase/app-server/config';\nimport type { AuthorizationConfig } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server';\n\nconst authorization: AppConfigFactory<AuthorizationConfig> = defineAppConfig(\n  () => ({ plugins: [sharingRules()] }),\n);\nexport default authorization;\n```\n\nDuring setup the factory registers the settings item `authorization.sharing-rules` (group `authorization`, actions `read`, `create`, `update`, `delete`) and its routes under `/api/authorization/sharingRules`, which the running application documents at `/api/swagger/docs` under the `Authorization` tag. Without the factory in the configuration the plugin adds no API and no route.\n\n## Service API\n\nResolve `authorizationToken` from `@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server` in the owning provider or route factory. The rule API exists only when the factory is configured, so narrow the service before using it. `quotes` is the application's own composite and `org.team` its own subject type, both defined as the main Skill describes.\n\n```ts\nimport { selection } from '@nocobase/authorization/core';\nimport {\n  defineSharingRule,\n  type SharingRulesAuthorizationApi,\n} from '@nocobase/authorization/sharing-rules';\nimport type { DatabaseConnection } from '@nocobase/db';\n\nif (!('sharingRules' in authz))\n  throw new Error('Sharing rules is not configured');\nconst rules = (\n  authz as typeof authz & SharingRulesAuthorizationApi<DatabaseConnection>\n).sharingRules;\n\nawait rules.create(\n  defineSharingRule('proposal-handover', quotes.reference())\n    .title('Proposal handover')\n    .subjects({ type: 'org.team', id: 'proposal' })\n    .scope('edit', 'quotes', selection.records(['quote-7']))\n    .scope('submit', 'quotes', selection.records(['quote-7']))\n    .scope('submit', 'projects', selection.records(['project-3']))\n    .reason('Delegate this proposal to the team')\n    .build(),\n);\n```\n\nA rule is `{ key, resource, actions, title?, subjects, reason? }` and each action is `{ action, scopeKey?, selection }`, where `selection` is `selection.records(ids)` or `selection.recordAccess(key, params?)`; `all` is refused. `create`, `update(key, rule)`, `delete(key)`, `get(key)`, `list()` and `withTransaction(connection)` validate before writing. A rule on a composite names the data scope in `scopeKey` and affects only that composite action's branch; a rule on a `database.collection` omits `scopeKey` and applies across every branch that reaches the collection. Neither form shares related records implicitly or replaces field and relation capabilities, and unrestricted identities skip every rule.\n\nThe service is a trusted provisioning API. A custom HTTP caller must check the settings item with `requireSettings(authorization, 'authorization.sharing-rules', action)` and validate the rule with `validateDataScopeRule`, both from `@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server/extension`, as this plugin's own handler does. Through HTTP, a rule key may not be `options`, `subjects` or `records` (`RuleKeyInput`) and `SubjectsInput`, which refuses a subject listed twice, and a create or rename to a key another rule already uses answers `409 RULE_ALREADY_EXISTS`; a handler of its own gets the same with `assertRuleKeyAvailable` and `rethrowRuleConflict`.\n\n## Installation seeds\n\nUse this only after confirming the plugin is installed, registered and configured and its migrations have run. Read the main authorization Skill's `references/code-and-seeds.md` for `defineSeed`, the restricted seed context and the code/configuration boundary. Seeded rules are ordinary configuration that administrators keep editing; do not protect or re-apply them.\n\nBuild the rule with `defineSharingRule` in a portable seed-data module and persist it in two tables:\n\n| Table                                 | Columns                                                                                                                      |\n| ------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `authorizationSharingRules`           | `id`, unique `key`, `title` (`encodeAuthorizationTitle`), `resourceType`, `resourceId`, JSON `actions`, `reason`, timestamps |\n| `authorizationSharingRuleAssignments` | `id`, `sharingRuleId`, `subjectType`, `subjectId`, `createdAt`                                                               |\n\n`sharingRuleId` references the rule row's `id`; a fresh seed may use the key as the id, but a rerun must use the existing row's id. Each `.subjects(...)` entry becomes an assignment row, not part of `actions`. Create the referenced principals, teams and business records first, check the key and each rule/subject pair before inserting, and write the rule with its assignments in one transaction.\n\n## Administration and acceptance\n\nThe plugin contributes no page; an application that edits these rules builds its page on the HTTP routes, which are listed in the package README and each checks `settings:authorization.sharing-rules` with `read`, `create`, `update` or `delete`. Reading options never grants write access.\n\nRun the owning feature's route and policy tests for allowed and denied records, multiple grants and rule removal. Verify changes with a new request and inspect the same operation with `POST /api/authorization/inspector/decide`; a client snapshot does not prove that a row operation is allowed. Report the rules and subjects changed and the observed allow and deny outcomes.\n"}],"details":{}}}

Authorization 组合插件 grants、record access、subjects 与 request-scoped context；for() 产出请求内授权对象。

export class Authorization {
    resourceTypes = new ResourceTypeRegistry();
    /** Built in: composites and the reserved `composite` resource type. */
    compositeResources;
    recordAccess = new RecordAccessRegistry();
    constraints = new AccessConstraintRegistry();
    subjects = new AuthorizationSubjectRegistry();
    routes = new AuthorizationRouteRegistry();
    plugins;
    provider;
    compositeResourceRegistry;
    middlewares = [];
    constructor(options) {
        this.plugins = sortAuthorizationPlugins(options.plugins);
        const grantProvider = this.plugins.find((plugin) => plugin.grants);
        this.provider = grantProvider?.grants ?? missingGrantService();
        this.compositeResourceRegistry = new CompositeResourceRegistry(this.resourceTypes, options.onInvalidGrant);
        this.compositeResources = this.compositeResourceRegistry;
        this.installApis();
        const apis = {};
        for (const plugin of this.plugins)
            Object.assign(apis, plugin.authorizationApi);
        for (const plugin of this.plugins) {
            plugin.setup?.({
                ...apis,
                ...(options.connection === undefined
                    ? {}
                    : { connection: options.connection }),
                get grants() {
                    if (!grantProvider?.grants) {
                        throw new Error(`Authorization plugin "${plugin.id}" accessed grants without a Grant Provider`);
                    }
                    return grantProvider.grants;
                },
                resourceTypes: this.resourceTypes,
                compositeResources: this.compositeResources,
                recordAccess: this.recordAccess,
                constraints: this.constraints,
                subjects: this.subjects,
                routes: this.routes,
                use: (middleware) => {
                    this.use(middleware);
                },
                middleware: () => this.middleware(),
            });
        }
    }
    /** Adds a step that resolves the request's principal and subjects. */
    use(middleware) {
        this.middlewares.push(middleware);
    }
    /** Runs every identity step, then sets `authz` to the request's context. */
    middleware() {
        return async (http, next) => {
            const request = createAuthorizationMiddlewareRequest(http);
            await runAuthorizationMiddlewares(this.middlewares, request, async () => {
                if (!request.principal) {
                    throw new Error('Authorization principal was not resolved');
                }
                http.set('authz', this.for({
                    principal: request.principal,
                    subjects: request.subjects.values(),
                    ...(request.keyScope ? { keyScope: request.keyScope } : {}),
                }));
                await next();

E38 · packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/record-access.js:1–48

defineRecordAccess/RecordAccessBuilder 校验 key、collections 与 resolver，构建可复用的记录范围声明。

export class RecordAccessBuilder {
    metadata;
    resolveFn;
    constructor(metadata, resolve) {
        this.metadata = structuredClone(metadata);
        this.resolveFn = resolve;
    }
    title(title) {
        return new RecordAccessBuilder({ ...this.metadata, title }, this.resolveFn);
    }
    description(description) {
        return new RecordAccessBuilder({ ...this.metadata, description }, this.resolveFn);
    }
    collections(...collections) {
        return new RecordAccessBuilder({ ...this.metadata, collections }, this.resolveFn);
    }
    /** Declares the params type; the optional schema describes it to editors. */
    params(paramsSchema) {
        return new RecordAccessBuilder(paramsSchema === undefined
            ? this.metadata
            : { ...this.metadata, paramsSchema });
    }
    resolver(resolve) {
        return new RecordAccessBuilder(this.metadata, resolve);
    }
    build() {
        const resolve = this.resolveFn;
        if (!resolve)
            throw new TypeError('Record access requires a resolver');
        return validateDefinition({ ...structuredClone(this.metadata), resolve });
    }
    reference() {
        const { key, collections } = this.build();
        return { key, collections: [...collections] };
    }
}
/** Builds a definition; `authz.recordAccess.define` registers it. */
export function defineRecordAccess(key, configure) {
    if (!key)
        throw new TypeError('A record access key is required');
    return configure(new RecordAccessBuilder({ key, collections: [] }));
}
function validateDefinition(definition) {
    if (!definition.key)
        throw new TypeError('A record access key is required');
    if (typeof definition.resolve !== 'function')
        throw new TypeError('Record access requires a resolver');
    if (!definition.collections.length ||

E39 · packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/composite.js:5–44

CompositeResourceActionBuilder 组合 grants 与 data scopes，并拒绝重复 scope key。

export class CompositeResourceActionBuilder {
    data;
    constructor(data = { grants: [], dataScopes: [] }) {
        this.data = structuredClone(data);
    }
    title(title) {
        return new CompositeResourceActionBuilder({ ...this.data, title });
    }
    grant(valueOrKey, permission, metadata) {
        const contribution = typeof valueOrKey === 'string'
            ? permission?.bind(valueOrKey, metadata)
            : valueOrKey;
        if (!contribution)
            throw new TypeError('A permission is required');
        const built = contribution.build();
        const dataScopes = [...this.data.dataScopes];
        for (const scope of built.dataScopes ?? []) {
            if (dataScopes.some((entry) => entry.key === scope.key))
                throw new TypeError(`Duplicate data scope: ${scope.key}`);
            dataScopes.push(scope);
        }
        return new CompositeResourceActionBuilder({
            ...this.data,
            grants: [...this.data.grants, ...built.grants],
            dataScopes,
        });
    }
    build() {
        return structuredClone(this.data);
    }
}
/** Type-safe grants and rule targets for one composite. */
export class CompositeResourceReference {
    definition;
    constructor(definition) {
        this.definition = structuredClone(definition);
    }
    get name() {
        return this.definition.name;
    }

DatabaseResourceAuthorizer 对 read/create/update/delete 解析 collection、grant、字段与范围；未知资源或动作直接 deny。

export class DatabaseResourceAuthorizer {
    recordAccess;
    resolveCollection;
    constructor(options) {
        this.recordAccess = options.recordAccess;
        this.resolveCollection = options.resolveCollection;
    }
    /** db owns the metadata, so an unknown Collection is whatever db does not hold. */
    async collection(name, action) {
        if (!this.resolveCollection) {
            return this.deny('DATABASE_UNAVAILABLE', 'Database authorization was installed without a database connection');
        }
        const collection = actions.includes(action)
            ? await this.resolveCollection(name)
            : undefined;
        return (collection ??
            this.deny('UNKNOWN_DATABASE_RESOURCE_OR_ACTION', `Unknown database resource or action: ${name}.${action}`));
    }
    async authorize(request, grantsService, constraintsService) {
        const resourceId = request.resource.id;
        const resolved = await this.collection(resourceId, request.action);
        if ('effect' in resolved)
            return resolved;
        const resource = resolved;
        const params = request.params;
        if (!databaseCollectionFieldsKnown(resource, params?.fields)) {
            return this.deny('UNKNOWN_DATABASE_FIELD', `One or more requested fields are not registered for ${resourceId}`);
        }
        const resolvedGrants = await grantsService.resolve({
            principal: request.principal,
            subjects: request.subjects,
            resource: { type: 'database.collection', id: resourceId },
            action: request.action,
        });
        const grants = params?.operation
            ? resolvedGrants.filter((grant) => grant.origin?.resource.id === params.operation?.resource &&
                grant.origin?.action === params.operation?.action)
            : resolvedGrants;
        const configs = grants.flatMap(toDatabaseGrant);
        if (configs.length === 0) {
            return this.deny('NO_OBJECT_PERMISSION', `No database grant allows ${resourceId}.${request.action}`);
        }
        const fields = resolveDatabaseFields(configs, request.action);
        if (!databaseFieldsAllowed(params?.fields, fields)) {
            return this.deny('FIELD_NOT_ALLOWED', 'One or more input, output, filter, sort, or group fields are not allowed');
        }
        const reasons = grants.map((grant) => ({
            code: 'GRANT_MATCHED',
            message: `${grant.source.plugin}:${grant.source.id} allows ${resourceId}.${request.action}`,
            plugin: 'database',
            details: { source: grant.source },
        }));
        try {
            const constraints = request.action === 'create'
                ? []
                : await constraintsService.resolve({
                    principal: request.principal,
                    subjects: request.subjects,
                    resource: { type: 'database.collection', id: resourceId },
                    action: request.action,
                });
            const effective = request.action === 'create'
                ? { scope: true, configured: true }
                : await this.resolveEffectiveScope(request.principal, resource, request.action, configs, constraints, params?.fields);
            let scope = effective.scope;
            const resolver = new RelationPermissionResolver({
                resolveCollection: this.resolveCollection,
                resolveScope: async (collection, rules) => anyScope(await this.compileScopes(request.principal, collection, request.action, rules)),
            });
            const relationBranches = await Promise.all(configs.map(async (config) => {
                const relations = await resolver.resolve(resource, config.relations, request.action);
                return {
                    relations,
                    scope: request.action === 'create' || !Object.keys(relations).length
                        ? true
                        : configs.length === 1
                            ? scope
                            : (await this.resolveEffectiveScope(request.principal, resource, request.action, [config], constraints, params?.fields)).scope,
                };
            }));
            // Identical relation grants may union their root scopes. Different shapes
            // share one DB node, so conservatively require all contributing groups.

export const ownOrPublishedAccess = defineRecordAccess(
  'documents.ownOrPublished',
  (access) =>
    access
      .title(title('documents.recordAccess.ownOrPublished'))
      .description(title('documents.recordAccess.ownOrPublishedDescription'))
      .collections('documents')
      .resolver(({ principal }) =>
        buildFilter((filter) =>
          filter.or([
            filter.string('ownerId').eq(String(principal.id)),
            filter.and([
              filter.boolean('published').isTrue(),
              filter.boolean('confidential').isFalse(),
            ]),
          ]),
        ),
      ),
);

export const publishedPublicAccess = defineRecordAccess(
  'documents.publishedPublic',
  (access) =>
    access
      .title(title('documents.recordAccess.publishedPublic'))
      .description(title('documents.recordAccess.publishedPublicDescription'))
      .collections('documents')
      .resolver(() =>
        buildFilter((filter) =>
          filter.and([
            filter.boolean('published').isTrue(),
            filter.boolean('confidential').isFalse(),
          ]),
        ),
      ),
);

export const nonConfidentialAccess = defineRecordAccess(
  'documents.nonConfidential',
  (access) =>
    access
      .title(title('documents.recordAccess.nonConfidential'))
      .description(title('documents.recordAccess.nonConfidentialDescription'))
      .collections('documents')
      .resolver(() =>
        buildFilter((filter) => filter.boolean('confidential').isFalse()),
      ),
);

E44 · app/server/providers/documents.ts:290–360

角色分配在数据库事务内同步 permission set assignment 与 reader restriction rule，避免两步配置分离。

    async setUserRoles(userId, requestedRoles) {
      const roles = [...new Set(requestedRoles)];
      if (roles.some((role) => !DOCUMENT_ROLE_KEYS.has(role))) {
        throw new Error('Unknown document role');
      }

      const user = await database
        .connection()
        .query.selectFrom('user')
        .select('id')
        .where('id', '=', userId)
        .executeTakeFirst();
      if (!user) throw new Error('User not found');

      const subject = { type: 'user', id: userId };
      await database.transaction(async (connection) => {
        const sets = authz.permissionSets.withTransaction(connection);
        const existing = (await sets.listAssignments()).filter(
          (assignment) =>
            assignment.subject.type === 'user' &&
            assignment.subject.id === userId &&
            DOCUMENT_ROLE_KEYS.has(assignment.permissionSet),
        );
        const existingKeys = new Set(
          existing.map((assignment) => assignment.permissionSet),
        );
        for (const assignment of existing) {
          if (!roles.includes(assignment.permissionSet)) {
            await sets.revoke(assignment.id);
          }
        }
        for (const role of roles) {
          if (!existingKeys.has(role)) {
            await sets.assign({ permissionSet: role, subject });
          }
        }
        await sets.notifyAssignmentsChanged(subject);

        const rules =
          restrictionRules(authz).restrictionRules.withTransaction(connection);
        const ruleKey = `${DOCUMENT_READER_RESTRICTION_PREFIX}${userId}`;
        const shouldRestrict = shouldRestrictToNonConfidential(roles);
        const current = await rules.get(ruleKey);
        if (shouldRestrict && !current) {
          await rules.create(
            defineRestrictionRule(ruleKey, documentResource.reference())
              .title({
                key: 'documents.restriction.nonConfidential',
                ns: APP_NS,
              })
              .subjects(subject)
              .scope(
                'view',
                'documents',
                selection.recordAccess('documents.nonConfidential'),
              )
              .build(),
          );
        } else if (!shouldRestrict && current) {
          await rules.delete(ruleKey);
        }
      });
    },
  };
}

E46 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md:24–44

授权 Skill 要求先形成责任矩阵，再声明数据库权限/复合资源/记录范围，并保护服务端路由。

Choose delivery by installation state: use seeds for fresh-install defaults and authorized runtime services or a controlled data-change workflow for an existing installation. These are ways to apply configuration, not alternatives to model development. Reuse current management pages and editors. When the App owns a role editor, build it from the UI Library's `permission-editor` component (`yes n | pnpm exec shadcn add @nocobase/permission-editor`; its example is `@nocobase/permission-editor-demo`), mapping the registered business actions and levels to its sections and rows. Read the relevant references as needed, beginning with the business workflow for model changes and the service/configuration references for configuration changes.

## Start from business responsibilities

Before writing grants, turn the requirement into a matrix: actor/job, page, business action, allowed records, readable/writable fields, relation operations and exceptions. Identify who administers assignments separately from who uses the business feature. Ask only about missing decisions that affect access; record concrete assumptions and continue independent modeling work.

Use jobs as permission sets, such as sales engineer or delivery specialist. Ownership, region and assigned projects are record scopes, not extra role names. A person may hold a direct job plus an inherited team job. Define whether revoking one source should preserve the other. Describe each exception as a business fact: a delegated quote, a confidential project, or an active delivery team.

The sales workflow in [the business module workflow](references/business-module.md) separates quote preparation from regional submission responsibility: an engineer can edit their own out-of-region draft but cannot submit it unless the parent project is also accessible. This is a useful model for multi-table operations; copy the responsibility boundary, not its sample ids.

## Choose the implementation reference

| Task                                                                                                               | Read                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Build a feature from model through routes, assignments and verification                                            | [Business module workflow](references/business-module.md)                                                                                                       |
| Declare typed actions, scopes, fields and nested relations                                                         | [Fluent declarations](references/fluent-registration.md)                                                                                                        |
| Bind generated CRUD to business actions or choose a custom business endpoint                                       | [Repository route integration](references/repository-routes.md)                                                                                                 |
| Assign permission sets to departments, positions or another organisation structure                                 | The application development Skill's `references/organization.md`; it builds the organisation first when the application has none                                |
| Design who gets what across departments, department heads and cross-department work                                | The application development Skill's `references/organization/permission-design.md`; the core needs permission sets only                                         |
| Team or department subject registration, default/sharing/restriction design, protected assignments and diagnostics | [Subjects and administration](references/subjects-and-administration.md)                                                                                        |

const NAMESPACE = '@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules';
const APP_NAMESPACE = '@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization';
/** The configuration factory: sharing rules with their settings item and routes. */
export function sharingRules(options = {}) {
    const connection = new DatabaseConnectionHandle('Sharing Rules');
    const plugin = sharingRulesPlugin({
        store: options.store ?? new DatabaseSharingRuleStore(connection.resolve),
    });
    return {
        ...plugin,
        dependencies: ['settings', 'database', 'ui'],
        setup(authz) {
            connection.set(authz.connection);
            plugin.setup?.(authz);
            authz.settings.add({
                id: SHARING_RULES_SETTINGS,
                title: { key: 'resourceTitle', ns: NAMESPACE },
                actions: ['read', 'create', 'update', 'delete'].map((name) => ({
                    name,
                    title: { key: `options.actions.${name}`, ns: APP_NAMESPACE },
                })),
            });
            authz.ui.place({ type: 'settings', id: SHARING_RULES_SETTINGS }, { section: AUTHORIZATION_SETTINGS_SECTION, order: 20 });
            authz.routes.add(SHARING_RULES_PATH, createSharingRulesHandler(authz, plugin.authorizationApi.sharingRules));
        },
    };

export function restrictionRulesPlugin(options) {

        id: 'restriction-rules',
        effect: 'restrict',
        bySubject: true,
        allowAll: true,

import { RuleService } from '../internal/rules.js';

/** A resource holds one default-access rule; a second one conflicts. */
export class DefaultAccessConflictError extends Error {
    existing;
    constructor(resource, existing) {
        super(`${resource.type}:${resource.id} already has a default-access rule: ${existing}`);
        this.name = 'DefaultAccessConflictError';
        this.existing = existing;
    }
}
export function defaultAccessPlugin(options) {
    const service = new RuleService({
        id: 'default-access',
        effect: 'expand',
        bySubject: false,
        allowAll: true,
        onePerResource: (rule, existing) => new DefaultAccessConflictError(rule.resource, existing.key),
    }, requireStore(options.store, 'Default Access'));

E51 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/SKILL.md:6–40

共享 Skill 说明 grants/default access/sharing 先合并、restriction 后收窄，并只允许选择具体记录。

# Develop sharing rules

A sharing rule adds selected records, or a record access selection, for the subjects it lists, provided they already hold the action. Sharing does not grant the operation, fields, page access or related records, and it cannot select all records. Grants, default access and sharing combine first; restriction rules then narrow the result.

Read the installed `nocobase-app-plugin-authorization` Skill first for composite resources, data scopes, fields, server policy enforcement and inherited subjects. The package README at `node_modules/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/README.md` is the complete reference for the service, the HTTP routes and the exports; this Skill covers how to use them.

For sharing between departments, read the application development Skill's `references/organization/permission-design.md` first. A rule whose recipient is a department shares specific records with it; a viewer-relative department scope such as 本部门及下属部门 is computed for the viewer and cannot select another department's records. The guide's optional sharing section covers this plugin, and its core section says what remains with permission sets alone.

## Development workflow

1. Identify the real collaboration exception: who receives which records, for which actions, and why. Use explicit records for one handover; use a record access selection for a maintained region or team rule.
2. Grant the recipients the business action through a permission set first. Register inherited subjects and their active membership resolver through the main authorization Skill.
3. Inspect every data scope the operation requires. Quote submission needs the selected quote and its actual parent project. Add each action and data scope deliberately; view, edit and submit are different permissions.
4. Save a complete rule through the HTTP routes or the service. Keep record ids tied to their data scope's collection and preserve the other entries on update.
5. Verify the handover works, an unshared parent still blocks the workflow, restrictions still exclude confidential records, and sharing alone cannot activate a missing operation.
6. Revoke the recipient or the team's permission set and verify the next request loses only that source. Independent direct-user sharing and direct assignments must survive.

For example, a quote handover shares quote edit and submit plus the parent project's submit scope with a delegated engineer. Use the real record ids, never sample ones.

## Install

Register the default export of `@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/client` in `client/plugins.ts` and of `@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/server` in `server/plugins.ts`, beside the main authorization plugin, and run the application's migrations. Add the factory to the `plugins` of `server/config/authorization.ts`, merged with the application's other rule factories:

```ts
import { sharingRules } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/server';
import {
  defineAppConfig,
  type AppConfigFactory,
} from '@nocobase/app-server/config';
import type { AuthorizationConfig } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server';

const authorization: AppConfigFactory<AuthorizationConfig> = defineAppConfig(
  () => ({ plugins: [sharingRules()] }),
);
export default authorization;

    async disable(userId) {
        await this.requireUser(userId);
        const context = await this.options.auth.administrationContext();
        await context.internalAdapter.updateUser(userId, {
            disabledAt: new Date(),
        });
        await this.revokeSessions(userId);
        return (await this.get(userId));
    }
    async enable(userId) {
        await this.requireUser(userId);
        const context = await this.options.auth.administrationContext();
        await context.internalAdapter.updateUser(userId, { disabledAt: null });
        return (await this.get(userId));
    }
    async resetPassword(userId, password) {
        const user = await this.requireUser(userId);
        if (user.kind === 'service')
            throw new UserAdministrationError('SERVICE_ACCOUNT_NO_PASSWORD', 'A service account has no password; it signs in only with API keys.');
        const context = await this.options.auth.administrationContext();
        validatePassword(password, context.password.config);
        const hash = await context.password.hash(password);
        const account = await context.internalAdapter.findCredentialAccount(userId);
        if (account) {
            await context.internalAdapter.updatePassword(userId, hash);
        }
        else {
            await context.internalAdapter.linkAccount({
                accountId: userId,
                providerId: 'credential',
                userId,

            });

E57 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-users/SKILL.md:18–40

Users Skill 明确插件只提供邀请页、没有用户管理页，并列出 disable/enable/revokeSessions API 与自定义页面建议。

  `@nocobase/app-plugin-users/client`. It contributes only the public
  invitation page at `/invite/:token`; there is no user management page.
- Typed API client: `UsersClient` and its types from
  `@nocobase/app-plugin-users/client/user-client`.
- Server contracts: `userManagementServiceToken`,
  `userRoleScopeRegistryToken`, `UserRoleScope`, and related types from
  `@nocobase/app-plugin-users/server/tokens`.
- HTTP API: `GET /api/users` (`page`, `pageSize`, `q`, `status`, `roleScope`, `role`; answers `{ data, meta: { page, pageSize, total } }`), `GET /api/users/options`, `POST /api/users`, `PATCH /api/users/:userId`, `DELETE /api/users/:userId?confirm=true`, `POST /api/users/:userId/disable|enable|resetPassword|revokeSessions`, and `PUT /api/users/:userId/roleScopes/:scope`. The running application documents each one, with request and response schemas, at `/api/swagger/docs` (JSON at `/api/swagger`) under the `Users` tag. Failures use the standard error body; branch on `error.reason`, such as `USER_NOT_FOUND`, `SELF_DELETE_NOT_ALLOWED`, `LAST_ASSIGNMENT` or `USER_EMAIL_CONFLICT`.

Every HTTP route requires Authentication and Authorization. Routes check the `user` record type with one of `read`, `create`, `update`, `disable`, `enable`, `assign-role`, `reset-password`, `revoke-sessions` or `delete`. Account creation checks both `create` and `assign-role`. `delete` is permitted only while an application role scope can clean a deleted user up.

## Register the plugin

1. Register Authentication, Authorization, and then Users in the App's Client
   and Server plugin arrays. The Client factory takes no placement options:
   `users()`.
2. Grant only the `user` actions those roles need, on `{ type: 'user', id: '*' }` because `user` is a record type. The plugin creates no roles and grants no access by itself.
3. For a user management page, build it in the App on `UsersClient` and declare it among the App's own routes with the page access it needs; the plugin ships none.

The invitation page reports results through `useToaster()` from `@nocobase/app-client`, so the App needs the `@nocobase/app-client` that exports it and registers a toaster service, as the templates do: `client/lib/toaster.ts` from the template, and `this.app.container.instance(toasterToken, createToaster())` in the `register()` of `client/service-provider.ts`, with the `Toaster` component mounted in `client/react-providers.ts`. Without the registration nothing throws, but its toasts are only logged to the browser console.

The default `app` permission-set scope is supplied by Users whenever the Authorization plugin's `authorizationToken` is available. Do not copy a user-roles Provider into an application. Set `users.permissionSets: false` to replace the default with an application-owned scope.

E60 · packages/@nocobase/service-provider/dist/registry.js:1–51

ServiceProviderRegistry 按 new→registered→booted→started 推进阶段，启动失败会标记 failed 并 shutdown。

export class ServiceProviderRegistry {
    providers = [];
    phase = 'new';
    shutdownPromise;
    add(provider) {
        if (this.phase !== 'new') {
            throw new Error('Providers can only be added before registration.');
        }
        if (this.providers.some(({ provider: item }) => item.name === provider.name)) {
            throw new Error(`Service provider "${provider.name}" is already registered.`);
        }
        this.providers.push({ provider, state: 'new' });
    }
    registerAll() {
        this.assertPhase('new');
        for (const entry of this.providers) {
            entry.state = 'registering';
            try {
                entry.provider.register();
                entry.state = 'registered';
            }
            catch (error) {
                entry.state = 'failed';
                this.phase = 'failed';
                throw error;
            }
        }
        this.phase = 'registered';
    }
    async bootAll() {
        this.assertPhase('registered');
        await this.runPhase('booting', 'booted', (provider) => provider.boot());
        this.phase = 'booted';
    }
    async startAll() {
        this.assertPhase('booted');
        await this.runPhase('starting', 'started', (provider) => provider.start());
        this.phase = 'started';
    }
    async readyAll() {
        this.assertPhase('started');
        await this.runPhase('readying', 'ready', (provider) => provider.ready());
        this.phase = 'ready';
    }
    shutdown() {
        this.shutdownPromise ??= this.shutdownAll();
        return this.shutdownPromise;
    }
    async runPhase(runningState, completedState, run) {
        for (const entry of this.providers) {
            if (entry.state === 'shutdown') {

原文件 SHA-256：1e91f78bcbcbfa82c25fe8b7f7126699cd13b413ec08b762e6bb3dd548897cfd

E63 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md:24–60

指引展示 provider 注册 token、boot 解析其他服务和生命周期表，但没有说明部分插件运行时中如何保护可选插件 token。

}

export const orderServiceToken=[REDACTED] =
  createServiceToken<OrderService>('app/order-service');

export default class OrderProvider extends ServiceProvider<Application> {
  public readonly name: string = 'app/order-provider';

  public override register(): void {
    this.app.container.singleton(orderServiceToken, () => {
      const database = this.app.container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
      return createOrderService(database);
    });
  }
}
```

Add the provider to the array in `server/providers/index.ts` and export its token from there so routes can import it.

Resolve it where you need it:

```ts
const orders = app.container.resolve(orderServiceToken);
```

**The token is the identity.** Two `createServiceToken` calls with the same name are two different keys — the container matches by object identity. Always import the token from where it is defined rather than recreating one.

`singleton` builds the service once, on first resolve. Use `instance` for an already-constructed value.

Keep the layers apart: a service should not read a Hono context, return HTTP status codes, or decide retry behavior. It takes its dependencies and returns domain results.

## Provider lifecycle

| Method       | Runs                             | For                                              |
| ------------ | -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `register()` | Assembly, before anything starts | Binding tokens. Do not connect or start anything |
| `boot()`     | After all providers registered   | Work needing other services                      |

E67 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-restriction-rules/dist/server/authorization.js:6–31

restrictionRules 工厂把数据库 store 绑定到 restrictionRulesPlugin，并注册设置项与 /restrictionRules 路由。

const NAMESPACE = '@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-restriction-rules';
const APP_NAMESPACE = '@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization';
/** The configuration factory: sharing rules with their settings item and routes. */
export function restrictionRules(options = {}) {
    const connection = new DatabaseConnectionHandle('Restriction Rules');
    const plugin = restrictionRulesPlugin({
        store: options.store ?? new DatabaseRestrictionRuleStore(connection.resolve),
    });
    return {
        ...plugin,
        dependencies: ['settings', 'database', 'ui'],
        setup(authz) {
            connection.set(authz.connection);
            plugin.setup?.(authz);
            authz.settings.add({
                id: RESTRICTION_RULES_SETTINGS,
                title: { key: 'resourceTitle', ns: NAMESPACE },
                actions: ['read', 'create', 'update', 'delete'].map((name) => ({
                    name,
                    title: { key: `options.actions.${name}`, ns: APP_NAMESPACE },
                })),
            });
            authz.ui.place({ type: 'settings', id: RESTRICTION_RULES_SETTINGS }, { section: AUTHORIZATION_SETTINGS_SECTION, order: 30 });
            authz.routes.add(RESTRICTION_RULES_PATH, createRestrictionRulesHandler(authz, plugin.authorizationApi.restrictionRules));
        },
    };

const NAMESPACE = '@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-default-access';
const APP_NAMESPACE = '@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization';
/** The configuration factory: default access with its settings item and routes. */
export function defaultAccess(options = {}) {
    const connection = new DatabaseConnectionHandle('Default Access');
    const plugin = defaultAccessPlugin({
        store: options.store ?? new DatabaseDefaultAccessStore(connection.resolve),
    });
    return {
        ...plugin,
        dependencies: ['settings', 'database', 'ui'],
        setup(authz) {
            connection.set(authz.connection);
            plugin.setup?.(authz);
            authz.settings.add({
                id: DEFAULT_ACCESS_SETTINGS,
                title: { key: 'resourceTitle', ns: NAMESPACE },
                actions: ['read', 'create', 'update', 'delete'].map((name) => ({
                    name,
                    title: { key: `options.actions.${name}`, ns: APP_NAMESPACE },
                })),
            });
            authz.ui.place({ type: 'settings', id: DEFAULT_ACCESS_SETTINGS }, { section: AUTHORIZATION_SETTINGS_SECTION, order: 10 });
            authz.routes.add(DEFAULT_ACCESS_PATH, createDefaultAccessHandler(authz, plugin.authorizationApi.defaultAccess));
        },
    };

E71 · app/server/routes/documents.ts:68–76

文档路由工厂在 authenticationToken 或 authorizationToken 缺失时返回空 Hono，与 provider 的 has guard 一起构成应用侧对部分插件 runtime 的绕过。

    // Every document endpoint is backed by the Authentication and Authorization plugins, which this
    // application always registers. A runtime that assembles the application's routes without those
    // plugins (the template's runtime tests do) mounts no document endpoints instead of failing to start.
    if (
      !app.container.has(authenticationToken) ||
      !app.container.has(authorizationToken)
    ) {
      return new Hono();
    }

E70 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/authorization.md:9–9

Default access, sharing rules and restriction rules are optional plugins. Read their installed Skills when the business requirement calls for a common baseline, collaboration exception or record exclusion. If the corresponding Skill is absent, treat the capability as unsupported in this App and explain that it requires separate development. Do not assume its APIs, tables or seed format, or silently install/configure it. If the Skill exists, follow it to verify runtime activation and migrations. Implementation and seed details belong to that owning Skill. Do not implement replacement role tables or assume all optional plugins are present.

What was delivered

业务场景与交付说明

单篇草稿临时共享

管理员仅将私有草稿 D 以 Can view 共享给乙，乙可查看正文且无编辑入口；撤销后刷新详情和重新打开资料页均不再出现 D 或正文。

保密资料强隔离

即使管理员将保密资料 C 以 Can view 共享给乙，乙刷新和搜索后仍看不到 C；负责人甲继续正常读取 C。

阅读与维护权限分离

乙只能阅读 P 和临时共享的 D，字段只读且 Actions 仅有 View；甲可编辑自己负责的资料，编辑弹窗标题、正文与发布/保密状态均正确回填。

停用账号即时生效

管理员停用乙后，乙原有登录会话刷新即返回登录页，重新登录提示账号已停用；甲的账号仍为 Active，三份资料完好可访问。

01管理员创建资料员甲和阅读者乙并分配角色
→
02甲创建公开资料 P、私有草稿 D、保密资料 C 并编辑 P
→
03乙仅能看到 P，详情只读且无编辑入口
→
04管理员将 D 以 Can view 临时共享给乙
→
05乙刷新后可查看 D 正文但仍无编辑权限
→
06管理员撤销 D 的共享，乙刷新和重新打开后均无法查看
→
07管理员共享保密资料 C，乙仍不可见，甲正常读取
→
08管理员停用乙，乙现有会话失效，甲账号与资料不受影响

Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

乙可以阅读 P 但不能编辑；甲可以维护自己的资料。

实际操作

  1. 以管理员在「Document administration」为资料员甲授予 Document maintainer、为阅读者乙授予 Document reader
  2. 以甲登录，在 Documents 新建公开资料 P，打开 Edit 检查标题、正文、Published、Confidential 均已回填，修改正文后保存
  3. 以乙登录并打开 P 的详情与 Actions 菜单

观察结果

  1. 甲编辑弹窗回填 Title=公开资料 P、Body=原文、Published=勾选、Confidential=未勾选，保存后列表显示「（甲已更新）」
  2. 乙列表中可见 P，详情正文完整显示，Title/Body 输入框均为 disabled，仅有关闭按钮
  3. 乙的 P Actions 菜单只有 View，没有 Edit/Delete
  4. 甲登录后可看到自己的三份资料，并保有编辑入口

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

02

乙原本不能打开 D；管理员开放 D 后，乙能查看 D，但没有获得编辑权限或其他草稿的访问权。

乙搜索保密资料 C 仍显示 No documents match your search，未因共享 D 获得其他资料访问权

A2 · 4 条操作 · 5 条观察 · 4 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

乙原本不能打开 D；管理员开放 D 后，乙能查看 D，但没有获得编辑权限或其他草稿的访问权。

实际操作

  1. 乙在开放前按标题搜索「私有草稿」，列表显示 No documents match your search
  2. 管理员在 Document administration > Sharing rules 用 Share a document 将「私有草稿 D」以 Can view 权限共享给阅读者乙并创建规则
  3. 乙重新加载 Documents，打开 D 详情，并查看 D 的 Actions 菜单
  4. 乙搜索「保密资料」确认 C 仍不可见

观察结果

  1. 开放前乙的列表只有公开资料 P，搜索 D 返回 No documents match your search
  2. 共享规则创建后列表显示 Rule=document-share-mvedo9o0、Shared with=阅读者乙、Document=私有草稿 D、Permission=Can view
  3. 乙刷新后列表出现私有草稿 D，详情正文「这是私有草稿 D 的正文，未发布前只有负责人可见。」可读，字段均 disabled
  4. 乙对 D 的 Actions 只有 View，没有 Edit/Delete
  5. 乙搜索保密资料 C 仍显示 No documents match your search，未因共享 D 获得其他资料访问权

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

03

撤销 D 的开放后，乙刷新详情或再次打开原链接，都看不到正文。

D 正文「这是私有草稿 D 的正文，未发布前只有负责人可见。」在撤销后无法再通过页面看到

A3 · 2 条操作 · 4 条观察 · 2 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

撤销 D 的开放后，乙刷新详情或再次打开原链接，都看不到正文。

实际操作

  1. 乙保持私有草稿 D 详情打开时，管理员在 Sharing rules 删除该共享规则并确认
  2. 乙在详情打开状态刷新页面，随后再次打开 /main/documents 原链接并搜索「私有草稿」

观察结果

  1. 管理员删除规则后提示 Sharing rule deleted，Sharing rules 列表恢复为空
  2. 乙刷新后页面跳回 Documents 列表，D 已从列表消失，详情正文不再出现
  3. 乙再次打开原链接后列表仍只有公开资料 P，搜索「私有草稿」返回 No documents match your search
  4. D 正文「这是私有草稿 D 的正文，未发布前只有负责人可见。」在撤销后无法再通过页面看到

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

04

即使给乙开放了 C，乙仍看不到保密内容，甲仍可正常读取。

甲登录后列表可见保密资料 C（Published Confidential），打开详情正文「这是保密资料 C 的正文，仅负责人和管理员可见。」正常显示

A4 · 3 条操作 · 3 条观察 · 3 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

即使给乙开放了 C，乙仍看不到保密内容，甲仍可正常读取。

实际操作

  1. 管理员在 Sharing rules 用 Share a document 将「保密资料 C」以 Can view 权限共享给阅读者乙并创建规则
  2. 乙刷新 Documents 并搜索「保密资料」
  3. 甲登录打开保密资料 C 的详情

观察结果

  1. 共享规则列表显示 Shared with=阅读者乙、Document=保密资料 C、Permission=Can view
  2. 乙刷新后列表仍只有公开资料 P，搜索「保密资料」返回 No documents match your search，正文不可见
  3. 甲登录后列表可见保密资料 C（Published Confidential），打开详情正文「这是保密资料 C 的正文，仅负责人和管理员可见。」正常显示

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

05

停用乙后，乙原有会话无法继续访问；甲的账号和资料不受影响。

甲重新登录成功，Documents 列表仍可见公开资料 P（含此前修改）、私有草稿 D、保密资料 C 三份资料，账号与资料未受影响

A5 · 3 条操作 · 4 条观察 · 4 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

停用乙后，乙原有会话无法继续访问；甲的账号和资料不受影响。

实际操作

  1. 乙保持登录并停留在公开资料 P 详情时，管理员在 Document administration > People and roles 对阅读者乙执行 Disable account 并确认
  2. 乙在原有会话刷新页面，并尝试用原账号密码重新登录
  3. 甲退出后重新登录并打开 Documents

观察结果

  1. 管理员页面显示阅读者乙 Account=Disabled，并提示 Account disabled；资料员甲仍为 Active
  2. 乙原有会话刷新后跳转到 /main/login，登录页不再保留业务访问
  3. 乙重新登录被拒，页面提示 This account is disabled. Contact your administrator.
  4. 甲重新登录成功，Documents 列表仍可见公开资料 P（含此前修改）、私有草稿 D、保密资料 C 三份资料，账号与资料未受影响

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

完整原始验收要求
B01. 乙可以阅读 P 但不能编辑；甲可以维护自己的资料。

B02. 乙原本不能打开 D；管理员开放 D 后，乙能查看 D，但没有获得编辑权限或其他草稿的访问权。

B03. 撤销 D 的开放后，乙刷新详情或再次打开原链接，都看不到正文。

B04. 即使给乙开放了 C，乙仍看不到保密内容，甲仍可正常读取。

B05. 停用乙后，乙原有会话无法继续访问；甲的账号和资料不受影响。
查看原始验收摘要与逐条数据

B01-B05 全部通过。应用初始仅有管理员账号且无示例资料，已通过管理员 UI 创建资料员甲、阅读者乙并授予角色，由甲创建 P/D/C 后完成权限、临时共享、撤销、保密隔离和停用账号验证；乙原有会话在停用后立即失效，甲账号与资料不受影响。
